**Done items leave this file (Q16, 2026-09-26):** a ticked `- [x]` item is moved
verbatim to the dated archive docs/archive/OPEN-done-YYYY-MM.md by
`npm run inventories:refresh` (scripts/archive-done.mjs); check:generated fails
while one is left here. The queue score, next free number and the done-item
guard check read this file AND the archives, so archiving never changes a count.

**`[~]` items say when they are done (2026-09-27):** put what is left as one or
more markers on the item, written out in full and ANDed together:
``done-when: sql `SELECT <one column>` => <expected>`` (read-only, first row),
`done-when: test <path>`, `done-when: issue #N closed`, or `done-when: pr #N merged`.
Only add markers when they cover everything that is left (a screenshot or a
review still to do means no marker yet). `node scripts/open-done-when.mjs` runs them;
the nightly `open-done-when` workflow opens one nightly-red issue listing the items
that are ready to tick. src/test/openPartlyDoneItemsSayDoneWhen.test.ts ratchets the
count of `[~]` items with no marker.

   `event:read` -> `gh secret set SENTRY_READ_TOKEN`. I'll wire the sync to
   that name, so it closes the "Sentry not synced" ledger item on the next hourly run.

   **ANSWERED 2026-09-26 (owner pop-up): "cut it when main is current".** Claude runs fastlane beta from main
   once the open landings are in and main CI is green; you install it and open the app once.
   cause is fixed and pushed (see Q82), but no phone has the fix yet.
   (a) **Cut a TestFlight build from main** (at or after the Q82 fix commit)
   and install it on your iPhone. The only builds with the AppDelegate
   forwarding are ce4d38d59 (2026-09-02) and later, and every build so far
   also has the boot race (it depends on timing, so it may not hit every
   launch). (b) **On that build: sign in, go to Home, tap "Enable" on the
   notifications pill, then Allow.** The pill only shows after you've posted
   or applied, or on a later launch more than an hour after install. If you tapped "Don't
   Allow" on any earlier build, iOS will not ask again: turn it on in
   Settings > Helpr > Notifications first, then cold-launch the app. Within
   seconds `select count(*) from push_tokens` should be 1 and
   analytics_events should show `push_token_saved`. If it doesn't, the
   analytics events say which step failed. (c) **Only if you run the app
   straight from Xcode on your phone:** those builds get SANDBOX APNs tokens,
   and prod sends to the production APNs host (`APNS_USE_SANDBOX` is unset),
   so APNs rejects them with BadDeviceToken and the sender deletes the row.
   TestFlight/App Store builds are unaffected. Leave this alone unless you
   test from Xcode. APNs credentials are fine: APNS_KEY_ID, APNS_TEAM_ID
   (digest matches P85MCK558V), APNS_BUNDLE_ID (digest matches com.Helpr)
   and APNS_AUTH_KEY are all set in Supabase secrets. Whether the .p8 key
   itself is valid can't be checked without a real token to send to.

   set those two flags; a member's own write is undone by the database. Since
   Q99 the switch only opens the attach area and turns on for real once a

   (b) replace each switch with an "Add license" / "Add insurance" button that
   goes straight to upload-for-review; (c) remove the switches and show the
   attach areas all the time.

7. **Turn off Supabase's GitHub auto-deploy to production (Q121). Top priority.**
   On every push to main, Supabase's GitHub integration applies new migrations
   (and deploys edge functions) to prod within about a minute. Our deploy
   workflow's safety checks (lint, replay test, destructive-DDL stop) run after
   that, so for the last 24h (24 migrations) they have not blocked anything.
   Measured from prod's own logs 2026-09-23 (Q117). What you do: Supabase
   dashboard > Project Settings > Integrations > GitHub, and switch off
   automatic deploys to the production branch (exact toggle name not verified).
   Until then, each new migration turns db-deploy red by design (the new
   provenance check), so the side channel stays visible.
   **ANSWERED 2026-09-26 (owner asked "whats the best option?"): switch it OFF.** Measured: db-deploy
   36261725456 (18:16Z) judged 124 prod versions since the cutoff, 108 with a db-deploy receipt and 16
   acknowledged side-channel applies, the newest of them 2026-09-23 ~16:10Z (Q194). So nothing has come
   in by the side channel for about 3 days, but that is not proof the toggle is off. Off is the only
   setting where db-deploy's lint, replay and destructive-DDL checks actually run before prod changes.
   Still the owner's dashboard click; the provenance check stays as the detector either way.

7. **Backups (Q45): the restore is proven weekly now; five things are yours.**
   **ANSWERED 2026-09-23 (owner pop-ups): (a) yes, Pro is paid and intended; (b) keep 14-day retention; (c) back up uploaded files EXCEPT id-documents, encrypted (work item Q147); (d) no PITR (no paid upgrade); (e) BACKUP_PASSPHRASE and ban_fingerprint_salt go in the owner's password manager (owner to-do).**
   Measured 2026-09-23. Nothing below is needed for the drill to stay green.
   (a) **Is the Supabase org really on Pro?** The API says `plan: "pro"`, and
   `supabase backups list` shows 7 daily platform backups. Every doc said
   "free tier". If you are paying $25/mo, you already have in-place daily
   restores for 7 days. If you did not mean to be on Pro, that is a bill.
   (b) **Backup retention is 14 days, not 90.** The repo caps artifacts and
   logs at 14 days (`maximum_allowed_days` 90). Raising it to 90 is one
   setting, but it lengthens EVERY workflow's artifacts, and on the free
   GitHub plan that can go past the artifact-storage quota (billed). The
   alternative is a monthly copy somewhere else. Your call.
   (c) **Storage files (photos, ID documents) are in NO backup**: 152 objects,
   about 10 MB (proof-photos 64, job-photos 61, message-attachments 18,
   user-documents 4, avatars 4, id-documents 1). The platform backup excludes
   them too. Backing them up needs a service-role key as an Actions secret and
   puts ID documents (encrypted) into a GitHub artifact: a privacy trade-off.
   (d) **PITR** (restore to the minute) is a paid add-on, about $100/mo plus
   Small compute. Without it, worst-case loss is about 24 h (platform) or
   24 h + GitHub's up-to-7 h schedule delay (ours). You said no paid upgrades,
   so this is only the measured gap.
   (e) **Two secrets that no backup holds.** `BACKUP_PASSPHRASE`: is there a
   copy outside GitHub? If not, losing the GitHub account also loses every
   backup. `ban_fingerprint_salt` (vault): it cannot be re-created, and
   without it every stored ban fingerprint stops matching after a restore.
   Want it (encrypted) in the backup, or kept in your password manager?

    **ANSWERED 2026-09-26 (owner pop-up): (a) below the rows; shipped as 12b3cd95e, then REVERSED by the owner
    ("No I don't like this") → "Back on top, space held" (option c). DONE 2026-09-26: the bar is back above the rows; while
    loading, an invisible, aria-hidden copy holds its space, sized from the last count this device saw
    (localStorage `helpr_inbox_hidden_unread` = "<userId>:<count>", read before userId resolves so the first
    skeleton frame already holds it; the per-user read landed ~15 ms late and shifted the skeleton, measured).
    GUARD inboxOpensOnActiveWithoutHidingUnread.test.tsx (order + held space + null-userId + other-account; red on the old code).
    Measured (Chromium, poster-e2e, settlePage): return visits 0 @375 / 0 @1440 in 8 of 8 loads; a device's FIRST visit
    still shifts once, 0.0589 @375 / 0.0216 @1440 (nothing seen yet to hold), now listed in page-settle.spec KNOWN.

    still served the bar on top when re-measured). WebKit shots ~/.lh-shots/mq28-top-{375,1440}.png (reviewed ok).**

29. **PR #1810 (Q392): which parish job-match alert design stays? (2026-09-26).** PR #1810 and main
    (20260926041132, Q225/V-008) both fix "parish alert arrives before early access shows the job", differently:
    (A) KEEP MAIN'S queue-only design (every send goes through the 1-minute sweep, so elite/no-delay users hear up
    to ~60s later; nothing sent inside the funding write) and ADD the PR's block check (`user_blocks`, both ways)
    and shared `job_announceable_to()` gate. A user who deletes the alert can be told again if the job reopens.
    (B) TAKE THE PR'S design: elite/no-delay users told inline at funding (incl. email via net.http_post inside the
    escrow write), a permanent per-(user, job) `job_match_queue` ledger so nobody is ever told twice, block check;
    main's `parish_match_alert_queue` and its sweep step are retired.
    Measured live (pg_get_functiondef, prod): neither `notify_helpers_on_job_post` nor `deliver_parish_match_alert`
    checks `user_blocks` today; `deliver_job_match`/`job_announceable_to` do not exist on prod.
    Not blocked by this: the PR's instant-match half, digest gating, export_my_data line, and Q401 (auth-page
    noindex) are being landed separately. The PR's old-timestamp migrations must never reach main as-is.
    **ANSWERED 2026-09-26 (owner pop-up): "A + never twice": keep main's queue-only send path, add the
    `user_blocks` check (both ways) and `job_announceable_to()` gate, AND write parish sends into the permanent
    per-(user, job) ledger so a deleted alert is never re-sent when the job reopens. Lands after the instant half.**
    **DONE 2026-09-26: landed as migration 20260926195608 (Q392 parish half).**
30. **press-every-control: press one representative per repeated list row? (#1582, 2026-09-26).** Run
    36230166945: /admin?view=jobs alone took 97 min (412 presses), mostly the same four controls on every job
    card. Landed without asking: shards now claim rows from one shared queue (Q725), so a heavy row no longer
    strands the rest of its shard. Still yours: (A) keep pressing every card (complete, slow; ~97 min a run on
    that view), or (B) press every control on the first N cards of a repeated list and only count the rest
    (much faster; a defect that shows only on card N+1 would be missed). Default until answered: (A).
31. **Group crews: three money rules (Q728/Q729, 2026-09-27).** Group jobs are off
    (GROUP_JOBS_ENABLED=false), so nothing is charged under these yet. (A) CREW_FEE_PAYS_UNCONFIRMED:
    a late-cancel fee is shared by a member the poster OFFERED the job to but who never confirmed.
    Built as yes (your Q407(13) answer); confirm. (B) CREW_COMPLETES_WHEN_HIRED_DONE: an under-filled
    crew completes when every hired member is done, and the unfilled shares go back to the poster.
    Built as yes; confirm. (C) New: when a crew dispute or an unfilled slot returns a member's share,
    only the share itself (budget + urgent fee) goes back. The platform fee, sales tax and any gift
    card on that charge are NOT returned. Should they be returned in proportion to the share?
    ANSWERED 2026-09-27: yes, pro-rata. BUILT (see the ANSWERED block above); guard src/test/edge/process-scheduled-payouts-crew.test.ts.

Only tests that read `fx.openJob` should skip; they already carry per-test `test.skip(!fx.openJob, …)`. The other 2 skips (expanding-search) are justified. (1) FIXED in branch ready/uncov-auth: a refresh that fails on the network made supabase-js send the publishable key as bearer while a session was still stored (the 10-09 21:45Z desktop burst: anon 401s, then a 200 refresh 0.6 s later); the client fetch now holds such a request (src/lib/signedInRequestGuard.ts, guard src/lib/signedInRequestGuard.test.ts, red with the hold disabled). (2) NOT FIXED, cause unknown: every ledger row behind this item comes from three phone sign-up bursts (10-09 01:38Z, 02:47Z, 13:36Z, Safari on iPhone, brand-new accounts). Edge logs: member-token requests succeed for 5-8 s after the email-confirm landing, then the publishable bearer, with NO /token or /logout call in between; error_logs rows carry no user and getUser answered "Auth session missing!", so the stored session itself was gone on the device. Not seen since: 0 permission-denied rows 10-10..10-12 across 6+ new confirmed sign-ups. Next step needs a device/browser repro of sign-up -> email link -> /home on iPhone Safari (no browser work in this lane). Review of the hold (lh-silent-failure, 2026-10-12) fixed in the same branch: recovery refetches the failed active queries on the next TOKEN_REFRESHED/SIGNED_IN after a hold, the project URL is matched normalised, the deadline helper re-arms while hidden and caps restarts at 3, the class guard also sees abort and sentinel timers (useMapKitJs moved onto the helper). LEFT (code-read only, not reproduced): realtime's accessToken falls back to the publishable key in the same window, so RLS-scoped postgres_changes can go quiet until a heartbeat after the refresh (fix: a realtime.accessToken callback that throws while a refreshable session is stored); and in a blocked-storage browser the sign-out floor clears localStorage but not the in-memory auth store.

No app error near the after-photo upload (error_logs 23:40Z-01:30Z); right after it the Helpr account went to Stripe Connect onboarding and came back without marking done; that account now has payouts enabled, so nothing blocks finishing it. Agents may not release or refund a non-test job, so it needs the owner: mark it done from both accounts (pays the admin account) or have it refunded. Then an admin taps Mark Reviewed on it in Admin > Stuck Jobs (resolve_stalled_job_flag): that is the ONLY writer of job_completion_nudges.resolved_at (measured in pg_proc), and both alerts' close rule (admin_queue_still_pending 'stalled-job') waits for it, not for the job's status.

(a) VISIBILITY: seed_jobs_hidden_publicly() is false on prod (feature_flags key unset), so an escrow seed would show on public Browse today (prod-seed.mjs:534 refuses escrow seeds for this reason), and flipping it true would also hide the row from helper-e2e. (b) UNHIREABLE: an escrow row with no PaymentIntent that press-every-control or a journey could apply to, hire and release would make release-payout transfer from the LIVE platform balance (no source_transaction). The poster presses Message Helpr, which goes to `/messages?jobId=<job>&userId=<helper>` (confirmed in the trace); openConvo fetched the thread's messages, but the page ended on the list at /messages?chat=1 with no reply box. Reads as a race that closes the placeholder thread when the network is slow. - [~] **Q427 MEDIUM** (audit bus NB-017 ⚑, native deep links, 2026-09-25; CODE FIX ONLY, NOT verified on a device or simulator — this cloud container has no Xcode/simulator): helpr:// and Universal Links now enter through ONE function, `routeIncomingUrl` in src/lib/deepLinkRouter.ts, fed by both `App.getLaunchUrl()` (read once at boot) and `appUrlOpen`. Measured from code, not device: (a) on iOS `getLaunchUrl()` returns `ApplicationDelegateProxy.lastURL` and the cold-start `appUrlOpen` is retained until a listener attaches (node_modules/@capacitor/app AppPlugin.swift `retainUntilConsumed: true`), so a cold start from a link ran the handler TWICE (two Browser.close(), two navigate()); now the pair is collapsed (same URL, other source, within 10 s), while two real taps still route twice; (b) the appUrlOpen listener sat at the END of useNativePushSetup's one try, behind six push awaits, so any push throw left the process with no deep links; it now starts first on its own chain. The `cancelled`/effect re-run mechanism NB-017 hypothesised was already removed by the Q82 fix (NAVIGATE_REF, 9a06e04ce). AppDelegate.swift application(_:open:options:) and (_:continue:restorationHandler:) both forward to ApplicationDelegateProxy (read, correct; no UIScene manifest in Info.plist, so these are the entry points); Info.plist registers the `helpr` scheme. ANDROID: there is no `android/` project in the repo, so no intent-filter or launchMode exists to check — Android deep links cannot work until one is generated (`npx cap add android`) and given a `helpr` intent-filter + App Links; not done here. Guards: src/test/deepLinkOneRouter.test.ts (appUrlOpen subscribed once and getLaunchUrl read once in all of src/, both into routeIncomingUrl; launch/appUrlOpen twin routes once in either order; every edge-function `buildRedirectUrl` target, bounced through helpr://, normalizes to an App.tsx route) and src/lib/nativePush.bootRegister.test.tsx "attaches appUrlOpen even when push setup throws" (red on origin/main 61b1cb451); all proven red by their @mutate lines. DEVICE STEPS STILL OWED (tick [x] only after these pass): build this code (`npm run build && npx cap sync ios`), install on the iOS 26.1 simulator AND a real iPhone. (1) NB-017's failing condition, 5 runs, baseline 0/5: `xcrun simctl terminate booted com.Helpr; xcrun simctl launch booted com.Helpr; sleep 25; xcrun simctl openurl booted 'helpr:///legal?tab=privacy'` — must land on Legal with Privacy selected every time, and prod `error_logs` must gain one `tags->>'source'='nativeReturn.browserClose'` row per run (the handler-ran marker). (2) Cold start from the URL, 5 runs, baseline 4/5: `xcrun simctl terminate booted com.Helpr; xcrun simctl openurl booted 'helpr:///legal?tab=terms'` — lands on Legal/Terms, and prod `analytics_events` gains exactly ONE `event='app_opened_from_deep_link'` row per launch (before this fix: two), with `properties->>'source'` = 'appUrlOpen' or 'launch'. (3) Warm: two more openurls after (2) both navigate. (4) Real money path on the iPhone, Stripe test mode: open the app from the home screen, pay a seed job with 4242 4242 4242 4242 — the sheet must close by itself and land on /payment-success. (5) Tap https://www.louisianahelpr.com/legal from Notes, app killed and app running: both open Legal in the app. Then `node scripts/audit-bus.mjs status NB-017 --set verified --by <you> --note "<counts>"` and fix NB-001 next (its own sequencing note). STATUS 2026-09-27 (lane P3): needs a sim or device deep-link run; not run. Stays [~]. STATUS 2026-10-02 (lane D): still needs a simulator or device run; nothing measurable from this lane. **SKIPPED 2026-10-05 (lane/code-tooling-1005): needs a simulator AND a real iPhone run** (the device steps above); nothing in code has changed since (src/lib/deepLinkRouter.ts guards still green). Not doable in this lane (no device; the one browser/device slot is the launch lane's). **STATUS 2026-10-11 (local Mac lane, measured): still not runnable on this Mac's simulator.** The app DOES boot on the iOS 26.5 simulator with a fresh bundle swapped in (React mounts; 2026-10-06's no-mount finding no longer holds), but every `simctl openurl helpr://...` (5 of 5 step-(1) runs) stops at SpringBoard's "Open in Louisiana Helpr?" prompt, and nothing here can tap it: Xcode 27 ships no Simulator.app on this Mac (it needs `sudo xcodebuild -runFirstLaunch`, an owner action), simctl has no touch input, and a link clicked inside the app's own WebView gets the same prompt. The sim build is also unsigned (no associated-domains entitlement), so step (5) Universal Links cannot work on it. Next: owner runs `sudo xcodebuild -runFirstLaunch` (then Simulator.app can tap Open), or run steps (1)-(3) and (5) on the owner's iPhone; step (4) cannot run while Stripe is live (no test card on prod). **RE-CHECKED 2026-10-12 ~23:30Z (proof6 lane):** nothing changed since the 10-11 Mac-lane note; no browser or device in this lane. missing: the device steps (1)-(3) and (5): either the owner runs `sudo xcodebuild -runFirstLaunch` so Simulator.app can tap "Open in Louisiana Helpr?", or they run on the owner's iPhone; step (4) cannot run while Stripe is live.

- [~] **Q1170 MEDIUM A returning visitor's token refresh runs in series before every read on the first screen (found by the Q1158 lab work, 2026-10-03).** supabase-js refreshes an expired access token only once the app bundle has downloaded, and every read waits for it. Measured (local build, prod data, poster, cold, Chromium, scripts/perf/cwv-lab.mjs --expired, 375 Slow 4G + 4x CPU): /home `auth/v1/token?grant_type=refresh_token` 4485-5310 ms, first read at 5357, LCP 6396 -> 6996 ms (+600); /jobs refresh 3899-4876, LCP 4924 -> 5672 (+750); at 1440 +~200 ms. A real returning visitor's token is always expired after an hour away, so this is the common real-user load, not a corner. Fix to design: start the refresh from src/entry.ts beside the JS download and hand the result to supabase-js through an async storage getItem (never a second refresh with an already-used token); web only, native keeps its Preferences storage. AUTH change: needs an lh-authz-rls review before it lands. Done when the --expired lab run matches the fresh-token one, with a budget for it in e2e/web-vitals/first-screens.spec.ts. **RECHECK 2026-10-12 (proof4 lane):** the entry-side refresh has landed: scripts/perf/critical-path-budget.json's 2026-10-07 note records "the entry refreshes a returning web visitor's expired token beside the download", entry 4.1 -> 4.6 KB gz, /jobs --expired LCP 5628 -> 4900 ms. NOT done by its own bar. Missing: (1) the --expired lab run matching the fresh-token one (scripts/perf/cwv-lab.mjs --expired vs fresh, 375 Slow 4G + 4x CPU, /home and /jobs), a browser run for the lead; (2) its budget in e2e/web-vitals/first-screens.spec.ts.
- [ ] **Q1171 MEDIUM Signed-in first screens ask for their data only after the whole app has downloaded (found by the Q1158 lab work, 2026-10-03).** Measured (local build, prod data, poster, cold, 375 Slow 4G + 4x CPU, scripts/perf/cwv-lab.mjs): the app first draws at 4.37 s on /home and the first Supabase read starts at 4.39 s; the feed lands at ~5.2 s, LCP 5.95 s. /jobs: first read 3.80 s, LCP 4.77 s. The guest /browse already starts its job list from the entry beside the JS (src/boot/guestJobsPrefetch.ts, Q206: data-start 3796 -> 589 ms); the signed-in screens (profile row + the page's primary read) have no such path. Fix: the same pattern for the signed-in first screens, URL held equal to supabase-js's by a test (as src/test/guestJobsPrefetch.test.ts does). Done when the lab LCP of /home and /jobs at 375 drops by the data wait and the KNOWN ceilings in e2e/web-vitals/first-screens.spec.ts are lowered. **BUILT AND MEASURED, NOT LANDED (lane-product, 2026-10-07):** a learned-reads replay (the app records the first screen's read-only REST requests per path and user; the entry replays them on the next load with the stored token, and the app takes each one once) is on local branch lane-product/q1171-learned-boot-reads (vacuity 20/20 killed). Lab A/B (local builds differing only in the entry's replay call, prod data, poster-e2e, 375 Slow 4G + 4x CPU, scripts/perf/cwv-lab.mjs, medians of 3): the reads did start at ~1.34 s instead of ~3.95 s, but (1) a FIRST-ever load has nothing learned, so the cold numbers this item and the KNOWN ceilings measure cannot move (/jobs 4896 -> 4876); (2) a return load with the persisted React Query cache (24 h) already never waits for data, and the replays slow the JS download: --return LCP /jobs 4068 -> 4452 (+384), /home 2464 -> 2584 (+120), app drawn 3917 -> 4320; (3) only with the persisted cache gone (--return --drop-idb) does it help /jobs, 4864 -> 4472 (-392), and /home is still worse, 2468 -> 2640. A visitor without that cache has almost always been away more than an hour, so the stored token has expired and the replay does not run at all. So it waits on Q1170 (an entry-side token refresh), and it should skip when the persisted cache is fresh. Not landed: it makes the common return load slower. cwv-lab gained --return and --drop-idb to measure these loads.
- [~] **Q1172 MEDIUM framer-motion rides two first paints: the dock arrives ~1 s after the page, and /home carries it before its first draw (found by the Q1158 lab work, 2026-10-03).** (1) MobileNav statically imports NavQuickMenu and SharedLayoutPill (framer-motion), so the lazy dock chunk waits on proxy.js (35 KB brotli): filmstrip of a cold /home at 375, Slow 4G + 4x CPU (~/.lh-shots/perf/film1-home-strip.png): title bar and skeleton at 4.4 s, dock at 5.4 s. (2) /home's route closure carries framer through BrowseTasksFeed -> SwipeableJobCard: proxy.js + animate.js (~40 KB brotli) download before its first draw (cwv-lab jsBeforeLcp). Fix: load framer on demand (the useFramerMotion pattern NotificationPanel uses, or LazyMotion), then start the dock's chunk with the page so it draws in the first frame; extend scripts/check-deferred-vendors.mjs from the boot graph to every first screen's route closure, red on today's /home first. Same paint-timing class as Q1158's page fades: OptimizedImage's `fadeIn` starts at opacity 0, which Chrome's LCP does not count, for job photos once the marketplace has them (not measurable on an empty marketplace). **FIXED IN PART 2026-10-04 (cloud/perf-first-paint, DRAFT PR; protection pending the lead's real-browser filmstrip and cwv-lab run).** (1) The dock: MobileNav no longer imports NavQuickMenu or SharedLayoutPill; DockPill draws the identical plain `<span>` (same element, classes, inline background) until src/components/mobileNav/dockMotion.ts has loaded, which useDockMotion starts once the page has settled (or at once on a long press, so the quick menu still opens), and never while the dock is hidden (guests, marketing pages). NavQuickMenuItem moved to its own file. (2) /home: SwipeableJobCard's framer-motion gesture is its own chunk (SwipeMotionLayer), loaded on page settle or the first touch of a card; until then the card is its content in a plain `relative z-10` div, and the content is portaled into one DOM node React never recreates, so the swap moves that node instead of remounting JobCard (no image-fade restart, no lost state; SwipeableJobCard.test.tsx asserts the same DOM node and state across the swap). Shared mechanism: src/lib/lazyModule.ts. Measured on the built bundle (npm run build, origin/main c68ac5f vs this branch; static closure of the route chunk, gzip): /home Dashboard 404 -> 360 KB (framer chunks proxy 39.1 + animate 4.7 + use-transform 0.7 + use-reduced-motion 0.2 KB gone), dock MobileNav 330 -> 289 KB (proxy 39.1 + AnimatePresence 2.0 KB gone), framer reachable from neither. GUARDS: scripts/check-deferred-vendors.mjs now walks the static closure of every ENTRY_ROUTE_CHUNKS route (read from src/boot/routePreload.ts, never copied) plus MobileNav and fails on a deferred package in any of them, with an exact two-way KNOWN_ROUTE_CLOSURE_VIOLATIONS (Messages, PostJob: Q1299); RED on the origin/main build (6 violations: Dashboard x4, MobileNav x2, plus the Q654 one), green after; vacuity: dropping a KNOWN entry, a stale KNOWN entry, a renamed ENTRY_ROUTE_CHUNKS, a static framer import planted in MobileNav and in SwipeableJobCard (rebuilt) are each red. Source half in vitest: src/test/deferredMotionImports.test.ts (@mutate x2, both red), DockPill.test.tsx, SwipeableJobCard.test.tsx, lazyModule.test.ts. NOT measured here: the dock-vs-page filmstrip and the 375 Slow-4G LCP/FCP (the lead runs those); the dock's pill no longer slides on a tab switch made before framer has loaded (after page settle, ~1 s), by design. Still open on this item: OptimizedImage's `fadeIn` starting at opacity 0 for job photos (not measurable on an empty marketplace). **RECHECK 2026-10-12 (proof4 lane):** the web build live is c4b1b5254 (= main). NOT done. Missing: (1) the dock-vs-page filmstrip and the 375 Slow-4G FCP/LCP run (browser, for the lead); (2) OptimizedImage's `fadeIn` starting at opacity 0 for job photos, which is still open.

- [ ] **Q1662 MEDIUM An under-filled crew job now hands back part of Stripe's card cost (found building the no-poster-fee change, 2026-10-11).** Since 2026-10-11 the poster's service fee IS Stripe's card cost on the charge (owner: no platform percentage for posters). process-scheduled-payouts still returns that fee PRO-RATA with an unfilled share (MQ31(C), feeReturnCents), and Stripe never returns its fee on a refund, so the platform now absorbs that share of the card cost on every under-filled crew (before, the 12% fee left room). Owner decides: keep "the fee, pro-rata" or withhold the card-cost fee on a crew refund like cancel_escrow does. done-when: test src/test/edge/process-scheduled-payouts-crew.test.ts LOW A won chargeback with mixed row outcomes gets one admin notice for the whole dispute (lh-money-escrow review of Q1291, 2026-10-12).** chargeDisputeClosed's admin notice picks ONE wording in priority order: a dispute with a refused row and a balance-short row is told "Pay it by hand" as a whole (the balance-short row would then be paid twice once the re-drive pays it), and one with a held row and a balance-short row gets only the payout-hold wording. Only multi-row disputes (crew jobs). Also unverified: Stripe likely caches a balance_insufficient refusal under the re-pay idempotency key for ~24h, so a top-up may take that long to land (heldTipRepay the same). Fix: list each row's outcome in the notice. GUARD when built: a chargebackClawback edge case with one refused and one balance-short row. finding: lh-money-escrow@7ef117d25#7

Fix: no fee and no strike when the Helpr (or a crew member) is under an open review or banned; a refusal trigger on cancellation_fee_transfers like refuse_payout_claim_while_held (or (d) above covers all hold-only paths at once). (f) A retained-ban user_bans row written during a review has banned_by = self, and the confirm (UPDATE user_bans ... WHERE banned_by = p_user_id) rewrites it to its own judgment, which can weaken a stricter identity judgment; the confirm should apply the strictest judgment recorded during the review (lh-authz-rls re-review of f2644839e). finding: lh-money-escrow@fbdfa47c7#4 **PARTLY FIXED 2026-10-12 (branch ready/todo-a-4): (b) and (d) done.** (d) admin_release_payout_hold refuses 'ban_review_open' while the Helpr's review is open (20261013000532; PGlite old RED / new GREEN 3x; class guard src/test/payoutHoldReleaseWaitsForBanReview.test.ts: every function that clears payout_holds refuses an open review or is the review's own decision), so every hold-only path (instant-payout, cash-out-credits, Stripe auto payouts, and the late-cancel fee paths of (e)) stays blocked until the review decides; the admin copy says so. (b) a release refused by trg_refuse_payout_pending_during_ban_review answers neutral, final copy (create-payment edge case). (a) DONE too: auto-tip-charge ages out a tip held by an open review past its 14-day window like a payout hold (recorded 'failed', the poster told once, in words that never name the review; edge cases in src/test/edge/banReviewFreezesMoney.test.ts). Review copy: the hold-release refusal says a lift releases the hold and a confirmed ban keeps it (lh-money-escrow review #11; a confirmed-banned account's system hold can still be released by an admin, as before). STILL OPEN: (c) owner decision (refunds/money-in for a poster under review), (e) the poster is still charged the fee and struck when the Helpr is under review or banned (their pay itself now waits on the hold), **(f) DONE 2026-10-12 (branch ready/todo-a-5):** ban_review_retained_floor (20261013001155) returns the strictest standing retained judgment applied during the review (auto-applied, uncleared, a live retained row, recorded or re-applied since the review opened, not a spent suspension; a no-end suspension outlasts a dated one, in any row order), and BOTH admin_confirm_ban_settlement and lift_ban_settlement_review take it as a floor (a lift clears the card/bank matches first). PGlite src/test/pglite/banReviewConfirmStrictest.pglite.mjs on prod's bodies (md5 checked): live 6 FAIL, fixed ALL PASS 3x; guard src/test/banReviewConfirmKeepsStrictest.test.ts (every review decision that writes the standing takes the floor first, and the floor's filters). lh-authz-rls re-review: clean, with three LOW follow-ups kept here: (f1) the 'reapplied since' branch reads retained_bans.reapplied_at, stamped for ANY account, so a pardoned match whose retained row survived the unban (retained_via 'deletion', 1 on prod) can count if someone else re-applies that row during the review; fix: stamp the match row itself in enforce_retained_ban's ON CONFLICT flip and drop that branch; (f2) PRE-EXISTING: a lift whose result is 'banned' writes 'banned' over the review's 'banned', so no settlement trigger fires and the account's jobs stay unsettled once the freeze ends (a permanently_banned floor settles as of now, not as of the review); fix: run confirm's explicit settlement when a lift ends banned; (f3) PRE-EXISTING: confirm's rung branch still uses COALESCE(original_expires_at, '-infinity'), so a dated rung can replace a no-end review suspension. Also: the admin console should show the status a lift returns (a lift can now keep an identity ban). finding: lh-authz-rls@df48cf219#1

- [ ] **Q1714 MEDIUM An exact retained-ban phone is an UNVERIFIED number, so a banned account can poison someone else's number (lh-authz-rls review of lane3/q1586, 2026-10-12).** Phones are typed, never confirmed by SMS. A member who saves another person's real number and then gets banned leaves that number in retained_bans; when that person later adds it (sign-up or, since Q1586, any profile phone change) they are auto-banned at once (enforce_retained_ban), where before Q1586 a later add was only an admin doubt-check. Same exposure the sign-up path always had. Options for the owner: verify a phone (OTP) before it counts as a ban key or before it is matched, or keep exact matches on UNVERIFIED numbers as a doubt-check. finding: lh-authz-rls@d1438994d#1

- [ ] **Q1727 MEDIUM The tax preview and the charge use different addresses (lh-money-escrow review of Q1578, 2026-10-12).** The preview prices the job ZIP (CheckoutStep.tsx ~166 -> calculate-tax ~120); Checkout taxes the poster's BILLING address (create-payment ~850 customer_update.address auto, no shipping address). Now that assembly/handyman are taxed (Q1578), a poster billed in another parish pays that parish's rate, and one billed out of state pays $0 with no alert (taxedZeroOnTaxableLouisianaLabor only fires for LA billing). Fix: give Stripe the job site as the tax address (Stripe prefers a shipping address), so preview == charge. GUARD when built: a create-payment edge case where billing != job ZIP and the session carries the job-site address, red first. finding: lh-money-escrow@6e679c20c#1 **TRIED 2026-10-12 (lane 1 money), NOT landed: the first design is unsafe.** Measured in Stripe test mode: a Customer's `shipping` beats its billing address for Checkout tax (billing Austin TX + shipping Delcambre LA taxed Louisiana). But writing the job site onto the shared Stripe Customer persists: Subscriptions and invoices also tax `customer.shipping` first, so a Pro/Plus subscription (txcd_10103001, taxable SaaS) and every renewal would be taxed at the last job site, and a ZIP outside Louisiana stamped as state LA may make Stripe refuse every automatic-tax checkout on that customer (customer_tax_location_invalid; unmeasured). Options for the next build: (a) Checkout without automatic_tax, with a precomputed "Sales tax" line from a tax.calculations call at the job site, committed with createFromCalculation on completion (what recurring visits already do); or (b) shipping_address_collection so the poster confirms the job address in Checkout. Either way, validate the ZIP is 700xx-714xx and send the same address shape to the preview (Q1657). **TRIED AGAIN 2026-10-12 (branch ready/todo-a-4), dropped before landing:** the build saved the job ZIP as customer.shipping before the session, i.e. the unsafe first design above; the lh-money-escrow review blocked it on the same grounds (create-pro-checkout taxes the same Customer with automatic_tax, so a Pro/Plus checkout and every renewal would carry the last job site; two overlapping checkouts can tax one job at the other's site). The next build is option (a) or (b) above.

- [ ] **Q1674 HIGH The land queue merges about one PR per CI cycle while five lanes open PRs faster, so the open list cannot empty (measured 2026-10-12 08:2xZ by lane 2).** 88 open PRs, 58 labelled land-queue; main's last merge 07:20Z and 2 merges in the 3 h before 08:20Z (28 in the 24 h before). 352 Actions runs queued against ~11 running (actions/runs?status=queued|in_progress); the 08:17Z Land queue step itself waited for a runner. By workflow, pull_request runs queued: Vitest 50, Secret Scan 29, Test 28, Mobile viewport 27, E2E happy-path 27, money-and-authz record check 26, Lighthouse 24, Bundle size 24. Load the queue cannot use: a PR's own checks run when it is opened and again when it reaches the head and is updated, and only the second counts; duplicate PRs carry the same work (fixpr/* replacements open beside the PR they replace: #2758/#2812, #2760/#2811, #2774/#2809 and #2774/fixpr/2774-q1463; lane 5's #2746/#2749 re-implement lane 2's Q1650/Q1651, #2744/#2747, which are DIRTY); push-event Secret Scan runs on fixpr/** branches that also have a PR run. A SECOND cause, measured 09:33Z: the Land queue advancer itself has not finished a run since 07:11Z (run 38216345179). Its last 100 runs (08:53Z-09:33Z) were all cancelled: 43 check_run, 50 workflow_run and 7 dispatch events, one every ~24 s, each joining the workflow-level `concurrency: land-queue` group and cancelling the pending run before a runner picked it up (the check_run ones carry 0 jobs: the CodeQL `if` is job-level, the concurrency is not). With runner waits of minutes, no advance ever starts, so once main moves no BEHIND head is brought up to date. Two jobs also hold runners while polling the shared-accounts lock: E2E real backend 38204573267 since 06:11Z and Press every control 38181873948 (release/testflight-03c50f6, started 2026-10-11 22:48Z). Options for the owner (changes the required-check flow, so not a lane's call): run the heavy PR jobs only for the queue head (keeping a cheap lint/typecheck on open), batch the head and the next N into one merge-train run, cap open PRs per lane, close the replaced PR when a fixpr/* replacement opens. done-when: test a guard that reads the land-queue workflow and fails when a queued non-head PR runs the full suite, plus the measured merge rate over a day at or above the PR open rate. STATUS 2026-10-12: an item merge glued the setup-fee build note (Q1725) onto this line; the lander moved it back (money train); this item itself is unchanged.
- [ ] **Q1687 HIGH Nothing can land quickly: GitHub Actions is ~10 jobs wide and 298 runs were queued at 2026-10-12 10:25Z (oldest from 09:13Z), mostly the full PR check set of ~50 open PRs.** Measured: 55 Vitest, 22 mobile viewport, 21 E2E smoke, 20 sensitive-commit-check, 19 Test, 19 Lighthouse, 18 bundle, 33 secret-scan runs queued; lane 2 PR #2835's checks were queued 50 min without starting. Three causes, worst first: (1) the land queue was livelocked: 98 of its last 100 runs were cancelled, its last success 07:11Z, because the workflow-level concurrency group let every unrelated check_run event cancel the pending advance (fixed in PR #2835, Q1674, which is itself waiting for runners); (2) every PR runs its whole check set when it is opened AND again when the queue brings it up to date as head, and under strict up-to-date the first run is thrown away for nearly every PR; (3) the same work is open twice under two PRs (e.g. fix-land/q1287 #2818 and fixpr/2771-q1287 #2806; fix-land/q1353 #2819 and fixpr/2772-q1353 #2807; fix-land/q1530 #2821 and fixpr/2775-profile-rows #2788), doubling those jobs. Proposal (needs the owner or lead, because PRs would stop getting early CI feedback): run the heavy PR checks only on the land-queue head (e.g. a `queue-head` label the queue sets), keeping lint/typecheck on every push; and land.sh refuses a second open PR for a Q that already has one. done-when: a land-queue PR reaches MERGED within 60 minutes of becoming queue head on three PRs in a row.
- [ ] **Q1676 LOW The schedule-change clash checks use an older copy of the double-booking rule (lh-authz-rls review of Q1326, 2026-10-12).** request_job_schedule_change (Q736) and respond_job_schedule_change (Q925) each carry their own overlap query, which misses a crew seat taken while the crew is still filling ('open' job, no jobs.helper_id) and ignores a crew member's own group_job_helpers.helper_completed_at; helpr_booking_clashes (20261012171958) counts both. None of the three compares a booking that runs past midnight with one on the next date. Fix: restate both RPCs from their newest definitions to call helpr_booking_clashes, and decide the past-midnight case. GUARD when built: a PGlite proof that a schedule change into a still-filling crew seat's time is refused, plus a pin that both RPCs call helpr_booking_clashes. finding: lh-authz-rls@40a4ec692#2
- [ ] **Q1722 MEDIUM A refunded taxed recurring visit stays reported to Stripe Tax as collected (lh-money-escrow review of Q1578, 2026-10-12).** charge-recurring-visits (~2017) records off-session visit tax with tax.transactions.createFromCalculation; no path calls tax.transactions.createReversal, which Stripe needs on a refund of a Tax-API transaction (only Checkout automatic-tax refunds reverse themselves). So a refunded, cancelled or dispute-split taxed visit over-remits. No taxable recurring series exists live yet. Fix: reverse the tax transaction on every refund of a visit that has one. GUARD when built: each visit refund path calls createReversal for a row with a tax transaction, red first. finding: lh-money-escrow@6e679c20c#2
- [ ] **Q1723 LOW The preview and recurring-visit tax calculations send a ZIP with no street, unmeasured for a ZIP that spans parishes (lh-money-escrow review of Q1578, 2026-10-12).** calculate-tax (~120) and charge-recurring-visits (~1271) send {postal_code, state, country}; the Q1578 live measurement used a full street address. Measure one split-parish ZIP both ways with a live Stripe Tax calculation (no charge) and send line1 if they differ. finding: lh-money-escrow@6e679c20c#4
- [ ] **Q1728 LOW A gift-card-funded taxable job collects no sales tax (lh-money-escrow review of Q1578, 2026-10-12).** create-payment (~520) prices the gift shortfall line non-taxable and a fully covered job charges nothing, so since Q1578 a card-paid assembly job is taxed and a gift-paid one is not. Needs the tax on the labor share charged or absorbed by rule. finding: lh-money-escrow@6e679c20c#3
- [ ] **Q1659 LOW OWNER DECISION Handyman work on a house (real property) is now taxed as repair of movable property (lh-money-escrow review of Q1578, 2026-10-12).** Q1578 made the handyman labor line "Repair of Tangible Personal Property" (taxed 11.25%); Stripe measured "Repairs to Real Property" exempt. That follows the owner's 2026-08-23 call to tax all handyman ("covered either way"), but tax collected that is not owed must be remitted or refunded. The durable fix is Q374's "work on a movable item?" question at post time. Owner: keep taxing all handyman, or build the question? finding: lh-money-escrow@6e679c20c#6
- [ ] **Q1724 LOW A lost chargeback on a taxed recurring visit leaves its Stripe Tax transaction reported as collected (lh-money-escrow review of Q1655, 2026-10-12).** Q1655 reverses a visit's tax transaction on charge.refunded; a dispute the platform LOSES returns the money through charge.dispute.closed (status lost) with no refund event, so nothing reverses it. Fix: the lost-dispute branch of chargeDisputeClosed calls the same reversal. No taxable series is live yet. finding: lh-money-escrow@dcbbd8299#6
- [ ] **Q1729 LOW A dispute split whose poster refund throws after the Helpr was paid sends no Slack page, and markFailed's write is unchecked (lh-money-escrow review of Q1425, 2026-10-12).** execute-dispute-split (~1487-1509) marks the execution failed and answers 502 to the admin, but pages no one; markFailed's UPDATE (~1899-1915) has no .select("id")/unwrapMutation, so a zero-row write is only a console.error. Fix: page money_at_risk on that path and check the write. GUARD when built: an execute-dispute-split case with a refund that throws after the transfer, red first. finding: lh-money-escrow@49836de17#2
- [ ] **Q1675 LOW A job whose duplicate $2 setup fee was already refunded refunds the $2 again if it is later cancelled or disputed (lh-money-escrow review of Q1346, 2026-10-12; never happened on prod: 0 duplicate_onboarding_fee refund rows).** create-payment cancel_escrow (~2040), void-cancelled-payments (~1499-1519) and admin_refund_dispute (~2687) start from pi.amount_received, which does not go down after settleOnboardingFee's partial $2 refund, so each would return the $2 a second time out of platform money (cancel_escrow's alreadyRefundedCents check skips only when the earlier refund covers the whole amount). Fix: subtract refunds already ledgered on the PaymentIntent (payment_refunds by stripe_payment_intent_id) from the refundable base in all three. GUARD when built: an edge test per path with a prior duplicate_onboarding_fee refund row. finding: lh-money-escrow@c4e1df1bb#4
- [ ] **Q1677 LOW A first-post checkout that LOST the setup-fee race may be unable to cancel when its service fee is under $2 (lh-money-escrow review of Q1674, 2026-10-12; code read only, never seen on prod).** settleOnboardingFee refunds a losing duplicate checkout's $2 and never stamps its job. A later cancel_escrow or void-cancelled-payments asks Stripe to refund capture − max(service fee, Stripe fee), but only capture − 200 is left on the charge, so when the service fee is under 200c (a job under about $17) Stripe should reject the amount and the cancel fails. Needs two first checkouts racing. Fix: subtract refunds already on the charge (amount_refunded) from the refund amount in both paths (overlaps Q1675). GUARD when built: an edge test per path with a prior duplicate_onboarding_fee refund of 200 on a small job. finding: lh-money-escrow@9b97652ce#7
- [~] **Q1732 LOW A dispute whose test-mode refund or transfer leg was RECORDED in the ledger (payment_refunds / payout_transfers) still cannot be superseded without hand SQL (lh-money-escrow review of Q1383, 2026-10-12).** execute-dispute-split writes execution_refund_id only after its payment_refunds upsert (and the transfer stamp after payout_transfers), so the usual test-mode failure (ledger row written, then a later step fails) leaves a ledger row that rpc_supersede_dispute_decision reads as money moved, and both test-mode clears (Q1280, Q1383) refuse on a ledger row by design. Unreachable today (0 disputes on prod, measured by the review). Needs: one admin RPC that removes a NAMED test-mode ledger row (checked against Stripe as a test object) together with its stamp, audited, CAS, with a PGlite proof; or an owner decision to accept hand SQL for it. finding: lh-money-escrow@7ca6b15fa#2 **OWNER DECISION 2026-10-12 (pop-up): an ADMIN BUTTON, admin-only, audited and idempotent; no hand SQL.** **BUILT 2026-10-12 (branch ready/items-a-series-refund-r2):** the unsettled admin dispute card whose split refused for "Stripe test mode" offers "Clear test-mode records and re-open" (a 10+ character reason, the biometric gate). It calls execute-dispute-split with action clear_test_mode_records (supabase/functions/execute-dispute-split/clearTestModeRecords.ts): every refund and transfer id the job's books (payment_refunds; pending/paid/reversed payout_transfers) and the dispute's two stamps hold is retrieved at Stripe under the live key, and ONLY "a similar object exists in test mode" lets it continue (a live object refuses with nothing changed; any other error is 502 retry; a transfer row with no Stripe id refuses). Then 20261012215235_clear_test_mode_dispute_ledger.clear_test_mode_dispute_ledger (service_role only; admin role and not a party; decided + failed/dead run; escrow held; no live or stamped settlement claim; no restored gift; COMPARE-AND-SET on the whole checked set; audit row first with every removed row in full) removes those ledger rows and both stamps, and rpc_supersede_dispute_decision runs as the admin (their own JWT) so the dispute can be decided again. Idempotent: pressed again it answers already_superseded, or nothing_to_clear then supersede if the supersede had failed. REVIEW FIXES (lh-money-escrow of 6b2434f20: 1 MEDIUM + 3 LOW; lh-authz-rls: clean): the job's ESCROW (PaymentIntent, else checkout session) must also be a Stripe test object, and a job with neither is refused (#2); the clear refuses first when the job has another open dispute (supersede would refuse), and writes execution_error starting "test-mode records cleared by an admin", on which execute-dispute-split refuses to split (plain 409, no markFailed) and the button stays shown, so a clear that commits without its re-open leaves nothing a retry could pay out (#1); nothing recorded and never cleared is refused rather than re-opening into a loop (#3, the loop itself is Q1769). PROOF: src/test/pglite/testModeDisputeLedgerClears.pglite.mjs ALL PASS (34 checks) applied 3x, 27 FAIL with NEW_MIGRATION=skip (before: the refund-stamp clear and supersede both refuse). GUARDS: src/test/testModeDisputeStampHasAWayOut.test.ts (Q1732 block, 9 @mutate), src/test/edge/clearTestModeDisputeRecords.test.ts (20 cases, 12 @mutate), src/components/admin/AdminDisputes.clearTestMode.test.tsx (7 cases, 5 @mutate). Left: a 375 and 1440 look at the new card block (no browser work in this lane), and the live check after db-deploy. done-when: sql `SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_proc p JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid = p.pronamespace WHERE n.nspname = 'public' AND p.proname = 'clear_test_mode_dispute_ledger' AND p.pronargs = 5)::text` => true, done-when: test src/test/edge/clearTestModeDisputeRecords.test.ts REVIEW FIXES 2026-10-12 (lh-money-escrow of 389a4d210, approve-with-notes; lh-authz-rls: clean): the clear refuses a job with ANY enrolled test account on it (409, before any Stripe call; an unreadable list refuses 500), because once ready/stripe-test-mode-r lands money already taken on a cs_test_ session runs in test mode when some party is a test account, so its test records are its books (#3b, narrowed from "every party" by the re-review of 5152e5f3f, #1; guard: clearTestModeDisputeRecords.test.ts, 3 @mutate); the clear always builds its Stripe client from STRIPE_SECRET_KEY explicitly, never a per-job mode, so a merge with the test-mode branch cannot route it to the test key (#3a); the card label no longer claims "no real money moved" before the server checks (#5). Whoever lands the second of these two branches must keep the clear on the LIVE key when resolving execute-dispute-split: write stripeSecretKeyFor("live") there (stripeKeyChosenByMode.test.ts refuses a direct STRIPE_SECRET_KEY read in a routed function, and that branch declares its per-job stripeSecretKey later; re-review of 5152e5f3f, #2).
- [ ] **Q1733 LOW Post a Job: tapping only an hour on an empty Start Time commits AM, so a poster who taps 3 and 30 gets 3:30 AM without ever choosing AM (seen 2026-10-12 on prod in Playwright WebKit 390x844, poster-e2e, nothing submitted).** src/components/TimePickerWheel.tsx fills the missing period with "AM" (`parse24(value) || { hour12: 12, minute: "00", period: "AM" }`, update() keeps parsed.period), and the AM chip then shows selected; the submit button moved on to the pay step with no AM/PM tap. Decide with the owner: leave the period unset until tapped (submit says "Pick AM or PM"), or keep AM. Asked for: a WebKit 390 look after the change, plus a TimePickerWheel test with @mutate.
- [ ] **Q1734 LOW The Posts dock badge counts pending applications on never-paid jobs that My Posts hides (measured 2026-10-12 on prod, helper-e2e at 375: badge 1, all five My Posts buckets empty).** src/hooks/useActivityBadgeCounts.ts counts pending, unviewed applications on the poster's OPEN jobs with no payment filter, while My Posts drops every never-paid job (jobIsUnfundedDraft). The case on prod is a seed (SEED Anchor bookcases + outlets, payment_status abandoned, one pending application); whether a real never-paid job can get an application is not measured. Fix: filter the badge's join on jobs.payment_status not in NEVER_PAID_STATUSES, with a test that a never-paid job's application is not counted.
- [~] **Q1735 LOW When Louisiana Helpr (an admin) ends a recurring series, are paid visits nobody is booked on refunded IN FULL instead of less the card processing fee? (lh-money-escrow review of Q417 (c), 2026-10-12; filed on the WIP branch as "Q1733", renumbered: main's Q1733 is the time picker).** OWNER DECISION 2026-10-12 (pop-up): YES. When Louisiana Helpr (admin, service or platform path, NOT the poster) ends a recurring series, paid visits with nobody booked get a FULL refund, card fee included; a poster ending it themselves keeps today's rule (Q808, fee withheld). BUILT (branch ready/items-a-series-refund-r2): 20261012233226_platform_series_end_refunds_in_full adds the server-only public.series_platform_ends (RLS on, no policies, REVOKE FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated), written by end_recurring_series_by_platform ONLY for kind 'admin' (a chargeback end is the poster's own bank dispute, so it keeps Q808; a series the poster already ended answers already_ended and records nothing); the admin end's notice says "refunded in full". charge-recurring-visits reads the row in the stale-visit sweep (withholdFee is false for it, even on an unheld date or a tagged intent; an unreadable row refunds nothing that run and retries) and in the mid-run series_ended refusal (refunds in full and tells the poster; an unreadable row keeps the fee withheld and pages with the by-hand step). NOT covered (corrected 2026-10-12 after the lh-money-escrow review of 389a4d210, #1): a series ended by a permanent BAN of the Helpr (end_series_for_banned_account) still withholds the card fee (its notices say "less the card processing fee", _shared/seriesRefund.ts), and so does a chargeback end; whether either should refund in full is the owner question Q1763. PROOF: src/test/pglite/platformSeriesEndRefundsInFull.pglite.mjs ALL PASS 3x, 5 FAIL with NEW_MIGRATION=skip. REVIEW FIXES (lh-money-escrow of 6b2434f20, #4): an unreadable platform-end record mid-run no longer tells the poster "less the card fee"; the sweep no longer settles a platform-ended visit refunded only less the fee (it pages ops to refund the rest); a test pins that the intent's fee-withheld tag is ignored once an admin ended the series. The table's column is end_kind, CHECK (end_kind IN ('admin')). GUARDS: src/test/seriesEndByPlatform.test.ts (@mutate on the admin-only INSERT, the table REVOKE and the in-full copy) and src/test/edge/charge-recurring-visits.test.ts (eight Q1735 cases, @mutate on withholdFee, owesTheFee and platformEndedMidRun). done-when: sql `SELECT COALESCE((to_regclass('public.series_platform_ends') IS NOT NULL AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_proc p JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid = p.pronamespace WHERE n.nspname = 'public' AND p.proname = 'end_recurring_series_by_platform' AND p.pronargs = 4 AND position('series_platform_ends' in p.prosrc) > 0)), false)::text` => true, done-when: test src/test/seriesEndByPlatform.test.ts. finding: lh-money-escrow@60cd5ff79#1 REVIEW FIX 2026-10-12 (lh-money-escrow of 389a4d210, #2): an on-session PAID row refused mid-run while series_platform_ends cannot be read is left 'paid' (not settled), so the stale-visit sweep reads the record again and settles it or pages until the fee is refunded (guard: charge-recurring-visits.test.ts, shown red on the old line, @mutate). Gaps left: Q1766.
- [ ] **Q1736 LOW Spent does not count the crew block fees captured from an uncaptured hold (lh-money-escrow review of Q1345, 2026-10-12).** void-cancelled-payments' fee-capture branch captures the cancellation fee PLUS the crew's block fees (crew_block_fees), but posterSpend counts that path as jobs.cancellation_fee capped at the charge, so a crew poster's Spent misses the block fees that left their card. Fix: posterSpend adds the job's paid/owed crew_block_fees on this path (or the cron stamps the full capture somewhere Spent reads). GUARD when built: a posterSpend case with a block fee, and a void-cancelled-payments crew case asserting stamp == (captured - blockCents)/100. finding: lh-money-escrow@7ef117d25#4 (Re-filed 2026-10-12 by the proof4 lane: commit b4dc2f1d3 lost this item's header and merged its text into Q1663.)
- [ ] **Q1737 MEDIUM A missing /assets/ or /fonts/ file now 404s, but the 404 carries `Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable` (found proving Q1720, 2026-10-12).** MEASURED with curl -sI on www (before 21:33Z, build c4b1b5254): /assets/missing-Q1663-x.js, /assets/notificationFeed-B4je1ZJb.js and /fonts/missing-x.woff2 all answer 404 text/plain with that header, because vercel.json's `/assets/(.*)` and `/fonts/(.*)` header rules stamp every response under those paths, found or not. A browser may keep a 404 with explicit freshness, so a chunk asked for in a deploy's propagation window (Q199) could stay a cached 404 under that URL. Not reproduced in a browser. Fix to weigh: limit the immutable header to files that exist (e.g. `missing` on the header rule, or a 404 page with no-store). GUARD when built: extend src/test/immutablePathsNeverServeTheSpa.test.ts, plus a live curl in a post-deploy check.
- [ ] **Q1738 LOW A superseded dispute on a card-paid job whose escrow is a Stripe TEST-mode object can never close (lh-money-escrow review of Q1383, 2026-10-12, measured live).** After a test-mode stamp is cleared by hand (Q1280, Q1383) a test-mode stamp and rpc_supersede_dispute_decision re-opens the dispute, the new decision has no way to finish: execute-dispute-split refuses the test-mode PaymentIntent at its escrow check, and rpc_settle_dispute_without_payment refuses any job with stripe_payment_intent_id or stripe_session_id set ('dispute_has_payment', read live with pg_get_functiondef). The job sits disputed/escalated with payment_status escrow for good and the overdue-escalation reminder keeps firing. The Q1280 migration's header (20261005173719) says such a decision "is closed with rpc_settle_dispute_without_payment", which is wrong for a card-paid job; the Q1383 header says so plainly. Unreachable today (0 disputes on prod, 2026-10-12). Needs: an admin close-out for a decided dispute whose escrow is a test-mode object (admin, not a party, audited; no Stripe call, since there is no real money), with its PGlite proof. GUARD when built: that proof plus a vitest reading the newest definition. finding: lh-money-escrow@2065e822e#1
- [ ] **Q1739 LOW The saved-only feed and the saved count disagree when a saved job is not on the loaded pages or is not shown at all (lh-silent-failure review of Q1701, 2026-10-12, code read, not measured on prod).** savedJobIds (the saved count on the bookmark, Dashboard.tsx savedCount) holds every saved id, while the saved-only feed shows only saved jobs that are on a page already loaded AND pass every feed cull. A saved job on a later page, closed or filled, filtered out, applied to, dismissed, or from a blocked poster counts but never shows, so the count can say 3 while the feed says "Nothing saved yet". A load-more-until-found fix was tried and withdrawn in the Q1701 branch: it pages through the whole feed for jobs that will never show, and useLoadMoreSentinel only re-attaches on a dependency change, so the auto-paging did not start on its own. Needs: a server read of "saved and still shown to me" for both the count and the saved-only list (or the saved-only list read by id). GUARD when built: a test that the count and the saved-only list agree for a saved job on page 3 and for a saved closed job. finding: lh-silent-failure@28e0cfac5#2
- [ ] **Q1750 MEDIUM Gift cards stay on the LIVE Stripe key for test accounts (found building test mode for test accounts, 2026-10-12).** create-gift-card-checkout still builds its Stripe client from STRIPE_SECRET_KEY for everyone, so a test account buying a gift gets a live Checkout (the nightly prod-gift-card spec skips it as LIVE_PAY). It was not moved to test mode because a gift can go to ANY email address: a test buyer could hand test-mode credit to a real person. Needs a recipient rule first (a test buyer may gift only a test account, or the gift is refused), then route it through _shared/stripeMode.ts. Money change: it gets its own lh-money-escrow pass before landing. GUARD: src/test/stripeKeyChosenByMode.test.ts lists it in LIVE_ONLY; moving it to ROUTED is the done-when.
- [ ] **Q1751 LOW Pro subscriptions stay on the LIVE Stripe key for test accounts (found building test mode for test accounts, 2026-10-12).** create-pro-checkout, check-pro-subscription, pro-customer-portal and subscription-reconciliation still use STRIPE_SECRET_KEY for everyone: Pro sells fixed live Price ids (PRO_PRICE_MAP in _shared/proTiers.ts), and a test-mode Pro checkout needs test Price ids chosen by the caller's mode (proTiers decides test prices only from the GLOBAL key's prefix today). Needs: test Price ids per tier and cycle, chosen by stripeMode, and subscription-reconciliation reconciling each mode against its own key. Money change: it gets its own lh-money-escrow pass before landing. GUARD: src/test/stripeKeyChosenByMode.test.ts LIVE_ONLY; moving the four files to ROUTED is the done-when.
- [ ] **Q1752 LOW Stripe Identity stays on the LIVE key for test accounts (found building test mode for test accounts, 2026-10-12).** stripe-idv-start, stripe-idv-webhook and verification-webhook use STRIPE_SECRET_KEY for everyone, and the identity webhook has no test-mode endpoint (we_1U5fInKp2H4b7tECFa6TwsPJ, test mode, was disabled in Q839). A test account starting ID verification creates a LIVE verification session (billed). Needs: route stripe-idv-start by the caller's mode, a test-mode identity endpoint with its own secret, and stripe-idv-webhook choosing the secret and key by event mode as stripe-webhook now does. GUARD: src/test/stripeKeyChosenByMode.test.ts LIVE_ONLY; moving the three files to ROUTED is the done-when.
- [ ] **Q1753 MEDIUM process-scheduled-payouts re-drives held tips and won-chargeback re-pays on the LIVE key only (found building test mode for test accounts, 2026-10-12).** Its per-job payouts now run in each job's own Stripe mode, but the end-of-run re-drives (redriveHeldClawbackRepays in _shared/chargebackClawback.ts, redriveHeldTips in _shared/heldTipRepay.ts) take one client for every row and get the live one. A TEST account's held tip or won-chargeback re-pay is tried with the live key, which cannot read its test-mode objects, so it cannot settle (what the re-drive then reports per run was not measured; no live money can move to a test-mode Connect account, which Stripe answers "can only be used with testmode keys", see _shared/stripeAccountUsable.ts). Needs: each row's job decides its client (createStripeModeResolver.clientForJob). Money change: it gets its own lh-money-escrow pass before landing. GUARD when built: an edge test with one test row and one real row, each re-paid on its own key (stripeMock.__keys). finding: lh-money-escrow@7389f2a30#3
- [ ] **Q1754 MEDIUM The e2e sweeper still settles hired+funded leftovers forward only when the repo variable E2E_STRIPE_MODE is "test", which means "all of prod runs the test key" (found building test mode for test accounts, 2026-10-12; `gh variable list` reads E2E_STRIPE_MODE=live, set 2026-09-28T00:41Z).** scripts/e2e/prod-lifecycle-sweeper.mjs (STRIPE_TEST_MODE, CAN_SETTLE_FORWARD) and the five workflows that pass the variable assume ONE key for all of prod. With test mode for test accounts, a cs_test_ row on the shared test accounts is settled by create-payment `release` on the TEST key whatever the live key is, so the variable no longer says what the sweeper needs to know; until this changes every sweep defers those rows (Q421's settled-escrow sweep log cannot appear). Decide: drop the variable for rows whose Checkout Session is cs_test_ (keeping settleRefusalReason's cs_test_ rule), or redefine it; update stripe-sandbox-on.sh/-off.sh and src/test/sweeperFailsClosed.test.ts with it. Money change: it gets its own lh-money-escrow pass before landing. GUARD: src/test/sweeperFailsClosed.test.ts (its E2E_STRIPE_MODE cases change with the rule).
- [ ] **Q1755 MEDIUM A test account can apply to (and be hired on) a REAL job, which puts a test account on real money (found by the lh-authz-rls and lh-money-escrow reviews of the test-mode branch, 2026-10-12; the 2026-10-09 incident was exactly this: the shared test Helpr applied to the owner's real job).** enforce_application_job_state rule C6 only stops a REAL user applying to a seed job (`v_job.is_seed AND seed_hidden_for(helper)`); nothing stops the reverse, nor a direct offer or accept between a real poster and a test account. With test mode for test accounts the money side is safe (a payout of live escrow to a test Helpr refuses as mixed_parties, and the poster's refund still runs in live mode), but such a job can never be paid out and needs a refund. Fix at the source: refuse a test account applying to, being offered or accepting a non-seed job (and a real account on a seed job, as today), in the trigger and the offer/accept RPCs. Data-model change: migration, PGlite 3x, lh-authz-rls review. GUARD when built: a PGlite test that a test-account application to a real job is refused, red on today's trigger. finding: lh-authz-rls@8e3b0e622#1 Note (lh-authz-rls review of 000ca9c1e, info #2, measured 2026-10-12: 57 test_accounts rows, all is_seed; besides 55 fixture emails they include the seed admin d3128db4 and apple-reviewer f2098932): the App Store review account pays on the TEST key, so on any REAL job it gets create-payment's 409 "This job pairs a test account with a real account"; App Review must be pointed at seed jobs.
- [ ] **Q1756 LOW Re-minting a test account's checkout leaves its old LIVE Checkout Session open until it expires (found by the lh-money-escrow review of the test-mode branch, 2026-10-12).** create-payment escrow skips reading a cs_live_ session on a job that now runs in test mode (the test key cannot read it) and mints a cs_test_ one, but does not expire the live one, which Stripe keeps payable for up to 24 hours. Measured 2026-10-12 by the reviewer: 0 of the 68 abandoned and 2 unpaid cs_live_ sessions on test accounts' jobs were ever paid; after deploy no new live session is minted for a test account. Expiring it needs the LIVE key on a test account's request (the one live call this design avoids); decide whether that is worth it. finding: lh-money-escrow@7389f2a30#6
- [ ] **Q1757 LOW An admin can rewrite a job's stripe_session_id, and that prefix now decides which Stripe key moves the job's escrow (found by the lh-authz-rls re-review of the test-mode branch, 2026-10-12).** Poster, hired Helpr and offered Helpr are all locked out of jobs.stripe_session_id (enforce_poster_jobs_money_lock, enforce_helper_jobs_column_whitelist, prevent_job_field_escalation), but admins pass every jobs lock and "Admins can update all jobs" is live, so an admin (the seed admin d3128db4 included while it holds the role) could set a mixed job's session to cs_test_ and have a test-mode payout mark it paid while the live escrow stays on the platform. Same trust class as any admin money action; close it by letting only the server (service role) write stripe_session_id. Data-model change: migration, PGlite 3x, lh-authz-rls review. GUARD when built: a PGlite test that an admin-role UPDATE of stripe_session_id is refused, red today. finding: lh-authz-rls@d01090420#1
- [ ] **Q1758 LOW A cancelled job that mixes a test account with real accounts returns the WHOLE cancellation fee to the poster and leaves the fee ledger rows pending (found by the lh-money-escrow round-3 review of the test-mode branch, 2026-10-12).** void-cancelled-payments refunds the fee instead of paying it when any fee payee is of the other Stripe mode (feePayeesShareMode). Two gaps: (1) crew_cancellation_fee_shares and crew block-fee rows stay 'pending' though nothing will pay them, so a Helpr-facing list of owed fees would show money that never comes: mark them waived with a reason in the same pass; (2) one test member on a real crew refunds the real members' shares too (conservative, but real Helprs lose a fee they were owed; the once-a-day alert is the only trace). Unreachable once Q1755 keeps test accounts off real jobs. Money change: it gets its own lh-money-escrow pass. GUARD when built: an edge test of a mixed crew where the real members' shares are paid and the test member's is waived. finding: lh-money-escrow@1d0efe142#1
- [ ] **Q1759 LOW charge-recurring-visits settles money ALREADY taken (a paid visit's refund, an open visit Checkout's expiry) as NEW money, so a mixed series is skipped instead of refunded (found by the lh-money-escrow review of the test-mode branch, 2026-10-12; 0 recurring_visit_payments rows on prod, measured by the review).** The stale/series-over loop takes its client with kind "new" from the parent's parties plus the visit's payer and Helpr (charge-recurring-visits/index.ts ~734-742), and uses it both to expire an open visit Checkout and to refund a PAID visit. That breaks the rule in _shared/stripeMode.ts (money already taken runs where it sits; a refund needs only the payer): a real payer's series with a test Helpr on a visit (the Q1755 entry path) never auto-refunds its live money (a defect every run) and never expires an open live Checkout. The mode also comes from the parties, not the visit row's own cs_live_/cs_test_ session, and create-payment's recurring_visit mode counts only the parent's parties while the cron also counts the visit's Helpr, so a visit Checkout can be minted in a mode the cron then refuses. Fix: decide expire and refund with kind "refund" from the visit row's own session prefix. Money change: lh-money-escrow pass. GUARD when built: an edge test of a mixed series whose paid visit is refunded on the payer's key. finding: lh-money-escrow@000ca9c1e#2
- [ ] **Q1760 LOW Admin bulk payout still sums TEST batches into the money it calls real (found by the lh-money-escrow review of the test-mode branch, 2026-10-12).** src/components/admin/AdminPayoutBatches.tsx: the grand total now leaves is_seed batches out, but the selection total (selectedTotal) and both confirm dialogs ("This moves real money and can't be undone here") still include them, so an admin selecting a test batch is told test dollars are real. No money effect (release-payout picks each job's key). Fix: exclude is_seed from selectedTotal and name test batches separately in the dialogs. GUARD when built: AdminPayoutBatches.test.tsx selecting one real and one test batch shows only the real amount as real money. finding: lh-money-escrow@000ca9c1e#3
- [ ] **Q1761 LOW A Stripe TEST-mode webhook event that names nobody on its own object is applied, and its handler then follows references with the test key (found by the lh-authz-rls and lh-money-escrow reviews of the test-mode branch, 2026-10-12).** testEventScope (_shared/stripeMode.ts) reads only the event object's metadata, client_reference_id, emails and Connect account owner; an event naming nobody passes, on the stated assumption that only rows a test payment wrote hold its ids. Disputes and refunds carry no metadata, and chargeRefunded.ts retrieves the PaymentIntent on the test key and merges ITS metadata into the refund notice, which picks the recipient from job_id / customer_id / payer_id (refundNotice.ts ~344-358): a sandbox refund of an old pre-live charge whose PaymentIntent names a real user would notify (in-app and email) a real person about sandbox money. Only someone with Stripe sandbox access can start it. Measured 2026-10-12 by both reviews (read-only SQL): 0 real jobs hold a cs_test_ session, 0 real jobs hold a pre-go-live PaymentIntent, 0 pre-go-live payout_transfers sit on real jobs; whether the 2-8 real profiles' stripe_account_id values are sandbox accounts was not determined. Fix: on a test-mode event, refuse any refund-notice (and any notice) recipient who is not a test account, and add a nightly check that no non-seed row holds a test-mode Stripe id. GUARD when built: a stripe-webhook edge test of a test-mode charge.refunded whose PaymentIntent metadata names a real user: no notice sent. finding: lh-authz-rls@000ca9c1e#1 finding: lh-money-escrow@000ca9c1e#4
- [ ] **Q1784 LOW The Q140 PGlite twin crashes before it checks anything (found building hidden feature switches, 2026-10-12, on branch fixpr/2761-hidden-switches as its Q1654; that item never reached main).** `node src/test/pglite/nullArgNeverAllows.pglite.mjs` passes "the class list parses (84 entries in the SQL)" and then stops with `error: function public.job_offer_cutoff(date, time without time zone) does not exist` (re-measured 2026-10-13 18:40Z by the lander on origin/main 74a7b4555 + small9b; on 2026-10-12 it stopped earlier on `relation "public.ban_settlement_queue" does not exist`). GUARD when built: the twin's own run, recorded in src/test/nullArgNeverAllows.test.ts's header.

- [ ] **Q1788 LOW Payment-success ignores the job's status: a job already in progress still shows 'Posted — YOU ARE HERE' and a 'View Applicants' primary (found doing Q1536's look, 2026-10-13; split from Q1785).** Measured on prod builds e80a4ca9d and c7e14d40a, poster-e2e, /payment-success?job_id=90aa6c8b-bf01-5430-ab29-ac182087ad5e (SEED Repaint a porch railing: status in_progress, payment escrow, Helpr hired and arrived): the stepper marks Posted as YOU ARE HERE (Accepted, In progress not reached) and the primary is View Applicants.
