- [ ] **Q1790 MEDIUM The App Store reviewer's demo password is committed in the public repo (found preparing the 1.0.4 resubmission, 2026-10-13).** fastlane/metadata/review_information/demo_password.txt holds the apple-reviewer@louisianahelpr.com password in plain text, and the repo is PUBLIC, so anyone can sign in as the reviewer account. Fix after Apple's review ends: rotate the password, store it only as a GitHub secret that ios-metadata.yml writes at upload time, delete the file and add it to .gitignore. GUARD when built: a vitest that fails when any file under fastlane/metadata/review_information/ named *password* is tracked.
- [ ] **Q1791 MEDIUM Deleting an account never revokes its Sign in with Apple token (found preparing the 1.0.4 resubmission, 2026-10-13).** Apple requires an app offering Sign in with Apple to revoke the user's Apple tokens when the account is deleted (App Store Review 5.1.1(v)). Measured 2026-10-13: no call to appleid.apple.com/auth/revoke anywhere in supabase/ or src/, including supabase/functions/delete-own-account. Fix: keep the Apple refresh token at sign-in (server side), and have delete-own-account call /auth/revoke with a client secret signed by the Sign in with Apple key. Needs that key from the owner. GUARD when built: an edge test that deleting an Apple-linked account makes exactly one revoke call.
- [ ] **Q1792 LOW ios-metadata.yml's "Land synced Xcode metadata" job fails, so synced Xcode settings never reach main (found 2026-10-13, run 38448252004).** The upload job succeeded (the App Store Connect metadata did change), but the job that opens the refresh PR failed on untracked vendor/bundle files from the Ruby install. Fix: exclude vendor/bundle (path or .gitignore) before the job checks for changes, and have it go through the one landing path. GUARD when built: a workflow guard test that the land job's change check ignores vendor/.
- [ ] **Q1793 LOW App Review gets only one demo account and no labelled demo job (found preparing the 1.0.4 resubmission, 2026-10-13).** Apple rejected 1.0 under 2.1(a) because content didn't load. The reviewer signs in alone and Browse shows only real members' jobs, so they can't try hire, message or complete without touching a real person's job. Fix: give the reviewer a second demo account plus a clearly labelled demo job they can apply to and hire on, kept out of real members' Browse (seed_jobs_hidden_publicly), and name both in fastlane review notes. GUARD when built: a prod check that the reviewer accounts can see their demo job and real members can't.
- [ ] **Q1794 HIGH App Review's Pro purchase will not unlock: verify-apple-iap refuses sandbox transactions (found by the lh-compliance-store review of the submitted 1.0.4 build 7119, c16b0f0dc, 2026-10-13).** supabase/functions/verify-apple-iap/index.ts:97-108 rejects a sandbox transaction unless APPLE_IAP_ENVIRONMENT=sandbox (_shared/appleAppStore.ts:147-152); App Review buys with sandbox Apple IDs on the production build, so the reviewer subscribes, the server refuses, and nothing unlocks (2.1). Prod has 0 profiles.apple_original_transaction_id rows, so the path has never completed end to end. APPLE_IAP_ENVIRONMENT's value was not read. Fix: accept a sandbox transaction only for known reviewer/test accounts (server deploy, no rebuild). GUARD when built: an edge test that a sandbox transaction from the reviewer account grants, and one from any other account is refused. finding: lh-compliance-store@c16b0f0dc#1
- [ ] **Q1795 HIGH Subscriptions must be attached to the version, and the Membership screen lacks Terms/Privacy links (3.1.2) (found by the lh-compliance-store review of the submitted 1.0.4 build 7119, c16b0f0dc, 2026-10-13).** scripts/asc/README.md (2026-09-06) lists 8 auto-renew subscriptions at MISSING_METADATA and 4 passes READY_TO_SUBMIT, and the first group must ship with an app version; whether they are attached to 1.0.4 was not checked. src/components/profile/SubscriptionTab.tsx has no Terms of Use or Privacy Policy link on the purchase surface, and the App Store description has no Terms of Use (EULA) link. Fix: attach the group + passes in App Store Connect, add both links under Restore purchases, add the Terms URL to the description. GUARD when built: SubscriptionTab test that both links render beside the purchase buttons. finding: lh-compliance-store@c16b0f0dc#2
- [ ] **Q1796 MEDIUM Job Boost is sold through Stripe inside the iOS app (3.1.1) (found by the lh-compliance-store review of the submitted 1.0.4 build 7119, c16b0f0dc, 2026-10-13).** The Boost chip (src/pages/posts/postedJobCard/steps/OpenStep.tsx:91-98) and the ApplicantsStates boost lever (:301-315) have no platform gate; JobBoostDialog opens create-boost-payment's Stripe checkout in an in-app browser sheet. A paid listing promotion is a digital good. Fix: hide boost purchase on iOS (or sell it via IAP). GUARD when built: a test that no Stripe boost checkout is reachable when the platform is ios. finding: lh-compliance-store@c16b0f0dc#3
- [ ] **Q1797 MEDIUM "Manage membership" always opens Stripe, even for an Apple subscriber (found by the lh-compliance-store review of the submitted 1.0.4 build 7119, c16b0f0dc, 2026-10-13).** SubscriptionTab.tsx:136-147 handleManageSubscription always calls pro-customer-portal. Fix: when the subscription source is Apple, open itms-apps://apps.apple.com/account/subscriptions. GUARD when built: SubscriptionTab test per subscription_source. finding: lh-compliance-store@c16b0f0dc#4
- [ ] **Q1798 MEDIUM Sign in with Apple has never succeeded on prod (found by the lh-compliance-store review of the submitted 1.0.4 build 7119, c16b0f0dc, 2026-10-13).** Measured: auth.identities has 0 rows for provider apple (Google 3). Native sign-in (src/lib/socialAuth.ts:166-176) sends an id token whose audience is the bundle id com.Helpr; docs/APPLE_SIGN_IN_SETUP.md lists only com.Helpr.signin as the Supabase Client ID. Not verified: the live Supabase Apple provider config. Fix (owner, Supabase dashboard): Client IDs com.Helpr.signin,com.Helpr, then one real device sign-in. GUARD when built: a nightly check that an apple identity sign-in succeeds (or alert on provider config drift). finding: lh-compliance-store@c16b0f0dc#5
- [ ] **Q1799 LOW UGC rules lack an explicit zero-tolerance statement and an objectionable-content filter on messages (1.2) (found by the lh-compliance-store review of the submitted 1.0.4 build 7119, c16b0f0dc, 2026-10-13).** Report, block and /support exist; no src text says zero tolerance for objectionable content, and the only prod scan is scan_application_contact_info. Fix: add the statement to Terms/sign-up acceptance and a basic message filter. GUARD when built: a copy test for the Terms statement. finding: lh-compliance-store@c16b0f0dc#6
- [ ] **Q1800 LOW App Store product-page header and search-results art for the next version (owner asked 2026-10-13; uploads lock once a version is submitted).** Drafts rendered from live screens at ~/.lh-shots/hdr (header-3840x1646.png good; search-3840x2560.png needs its headline moved off the phones). Upload both on the next version before submitting. GUARD when built: none (store asset); record the upload in docs/APP_STORE_REVIEW_SUBMISSION.md.
- [ ] **Q1801 HIGH Background location never compiled into the iOS app (found cutting build 7119, 2026-10-13).** ios/App/App/BackgroundLocationPlugin.swift is not in the App target (0 refs in project.pbxproj, 0 hits in the 7119 binary), yet Info.plist declares UIBackgroundModes=location and the purpose strings and reviewer notes promise screen-off sharing during I'm On My Way; enRouteLocation.ts silently falls back to foreground-only. Owner (pop-up 2026-10-13): keep the notes, fix the code for the next build; agent fix-bg-location is on it (ready/bg-location-plugin). GUARD when built: a vitest that every .swift under ios/App/App is in the App target's Sources and every local CAPPlugin is registered.
- [ ] **Q1802 LOW A recurring-series date a Helpr held BEFORE their saved birthday became under 18 is still booked by the cron (lh-authz-rls review of 30e7034c4, Q1586(c); 0 under-18 birthdays on prod 2026-10-12, so unreachable today).** Q1586(c) refuses every NEW series_visit_holds row for an under-18 member, but a hold that already exists (claimed while the birthday was empty or adult, then an admin corrects it) is still turned into a visit by charge-recurring-visits: it charges the poster and inserts the visit with helper_id set (a service insert, not judged), then the applications upsert is refused by trg_applications_refuse_under_18 and the cron pages ops 'Recurring visit created without its application row'. Fix: before charging, skip and release a hold whose holder member_under_18 (service_role may call it) and tell the poster the date is open again, never the age. GUARD when built: a charge-recurring-visits edge case with an under-18 holder: no charge, the hold released. finding: lh-authz-rls@30e7034c4#2
- [ ] **Q1803 MEDIUM OWNER DECISION: what happens when a member types a birthday under 13? (lh-authz-rls re-review of 44ca75e95, 2026-10-13; a compliance question, not authz).** Since Q1586(c)'s fix (owner 2026-10-13: a typed under-18 birthday is SAVED and Apply/Post grey out), the birthday wheels in PhotoPrompt and CompleteProfile go up to today, so a birthday under 13 can be saved, and the account is kept with its name, phone and address. That is actual knowledge that a child under 13 is using the app (COPPA). Options for the owner: keep the account greyed out like any minor; or close and delete an under-13 account on save; or refuse to keep the personal details until a parent is involved. Measured: 0 under-18 birthdays on prod 2026-10-12 (the reviewer's read). finding: lh-authz-rls@44ca75e95#5
- [ ] **Q1804 LOW The unauthenticated sign-up completion path can now SAVE an under-18 birthday on a brand-new account, which its owner cannot clear (lh-authz-rls re-review of 44ca75e95, 2026-10-13).** complete-signup's bodyUserId path (only within 30 minutes of account creation, never signed in, bio still empty) used to answer an under-18 birthday with a 403; since 44ca75e95 it saves it, and lock_under_18_birthday plus complete-signup's rewrite refusal keep it, so someone who learned a brand-new account's id could lock it out of Apply and Post until support corrects it. The same caller could already write an adult birthday and other fields through that path, and a new account's id is hard to learn. Fix: on the bodyUserId (no session) path, ignore a birthday entirely (a birthday comes only from a signed-in member). GUARD when built: a complete-signup edge test that a no-session call with an under-18 birthday writes no date_of_birth. finding: lh-authz-rls@44ca75e95#3
- [ ] **Q1805 HIGH Land the held phone half of Q1586(a) once Apple has decided on 1.0.4 (split out of a2-1u by the lead, 2026-10-14).** Branch ready/phone-save-after-review (listed in ~/.lh-landq/queue.hold-ready.txt) holds 20261013012700_phone_save_rpc_rate_limited.sql (save_my_phone, the rate-limited phone save; REVOKE UPDATE (phone) and INSERT on profiles from anon and authenticated; client_compat_floor() 2), src/lib/phoneSave.ts, CLIENT_COMPAT_EPOCH 2 and the screens' saveMyPhone calls. Build 7119 still saves the phone with profiles.update, so while it is with Apple this would show the reviewer a refused save or 'Update Helpr'. Until it lands, a member can still probe whether a phone number is on another account with no rate limit. Land it only after the freeze flag in release/ says frozen: false. GUARD: src/test/appReviewFreeze.test.ts fails it while the freeze holds; phoneSavedOnlyThroughRpc.test.ts and phoneSaveRpc.pglite.mjs come with it. finding: lh-authz-rls@d3a9253aa#1
- [ ] **Q1769 LOW A decided dispute on a Stripe TEST-mode payment can never be settled, even after it is re-opened (lh-money-escrow review of Q1732, 2026-10-12; 0 such disputes on prod, measured by the review).** execute-dispute-split refuses a test-mode escrow ("this escrow was paid in Stripe test mode", "this job's checkout ran in Stripe test mode"), so every decision on such a job fails again; Q1732's button re-opens one only when test-mode refund/transfer records exist, and Retry settlement loops. rpc_settle_dispute_without_payment closes only a job with NO payment on file. Needs an owner decision: close such a dispute as "test mode, nothing real moved" (a sibling of close-without-payment that requires the escrow to be checked at Stripe as a test object, audited), or leave them for hand review. Note (2026-10-12): once ready/stripe-test-mode-r (Stripe test mode for test accounts) lands, execute-dispute-split runs a job whose every party is a test account on the TEST key, so a decision on such a job can settle; this item then narrows to test-mode escrow on a job that is NOT all test accounts (pre-go-live sandbox payments). GUARD when built: a PGlite proof plus an edge test of the Stripe check. finding: lh-money-escrow@6b2434f20#3
- [ ] **Q1763 LOW OWNER DECISION Should a recurring series that Louisiana Helpr ends by BANNING the Helpr, or that ends because the poster's bank disputes a charge, also refund paid, unbooked visits IN FULL (card fee included)? (lh-money-escrow review of 389a4d210, 2026-10-12).** Q1735 (owner: yes, in full, when Louisiana Helpr ends a series, NOT the poster) was built for the ADMIN end only (series_platform_ends, kind 'admin'). A permanent ban of the Helpr ends the series through end_series_for_banned_account, which removes the date holds, so charge-recurring-visits reads the visit as an unheld date and withholds the card fee, and the live function tells the poster "less the card processing fee" (measured by the reviewer with pg_get_functiondef). Example: a one-Helpr weekly series at $100, the Helpr is banned, next week's paid visit is refunded $96.80. A chargeback end (the poster's own bank dispute) also withholds it, by design today. If yes for the ban: end_series_for_banned_account records kind 'ban' in series_platform_ends (widen its CHECK) and its notice says "in full". Money change: lh-money-escrow pass. GUARD when built: a PGlite case of a ban end recording the row and a charge-recurring-visits case refunding in full. finding: lh-money-escrow@389a4d210#1
- [ ] **Q1764 LOW A dispute split already running when an admin's Clear test-mode records commits can overwrite the cleared marker (lh-money-escrow review of 389a4d210, 2026-10-12; a window of milliseconds between the clear and the re-open).** execute-dispute-split reads the cleared marker (execution_error starting "test-mode records cleared by an admin") only at its first dispute read; the re-read inside the settlement claim does not select execution_error, and a run that refuses after the clear calls markFailed, which overwrites the marker. With the marker gone and no ledger ids left, the button then answers "nothing to clear" and supersede has to be pressed again. Fix: re-check the marker in the claim's re-read and never let markFailed overwrite it. GUARD when built: an edge test where the clear lands between the first read and the claim, asserting no split and the marker kept. finding: lh-money-escrow@389a4d210#3
- [ ] **Q1765 LOW On a series an admin ended, a visit whose card fee was correctly withheld BEFORE the end, and whose row is still 'paid' (its settle failed), pages ops every day to refund a fee the poster was not owed (lh-money-escrow review of 389a4d210, 2026-10-12, info #4).** charge-recurring-visits' stale sweep keys the full refund on the series (platformEnded), not on why the visit went unbooked, so owesTheFee is true for any refund below the full amount. The owner's wording ("paid visits with nobody booked get a FULL refund") supports refunding in full, so the page asks ops for money the poster is now owed; decide whether a fee withheld before the admin end stays withheld (then compare the refund's created time with series_platform_ends.ended_at). Money change: lh-money-escrow pass. GUARD when built: a charge-recurring-visits case of a pre-end fee-withheld refund on a still-paid row. finding: lh-money-escrow@389a4d210#4
- [ ] **Q1766 LOW charge-recurring-visits' unreadable platform-end path has three loose ends (lh-money-escrow re-review of 5152e5f3f, 2026-10-12; code read, 0 recurring_visit_payments rows on prod).** (1) An OFF-session charge refused mid-run while series_platform_ends cannot be read has no recurring_visit_payments row, so nothing but that run's one fail() line records a card fee that may be owed (the sweep reads only that table; index.ts ~1980-1989, 2066). (2) When that read fails and no platform-end row exists, the poster is never told about the fee-withheld refund: the mid-run notices skip on platformEndUnknown and the sweep's settle branch sends nothing (the charge.refunded webhook leaves fee_withheld refunds to the path that made them); fix: the sweep sends tellVisitRefund when it settles a fee_withheld refund with no refund:<id> claim. (3) In the platform-end case the sweep's alert says the refund was made "by hand" / "not by this function" though it was this function's own mid-run refund (amounts and action are right). Money change: lh-money-escrow pass. GUARD when built: charge-recurring-visits cases for each. finding: lh-money-escrow@5152e5f3f#3
- [ ] **Q1806 LOW A fully gift-funded job's late-cancellation fee is never paid to the Helpr (found doing Q1490, 2026-10-12; code read, not seen on prod: 0 redeemed gift cards on a job).** void-cancelled-payments prices the fee (computeCancellationFee, or the crew shares) BEFORE it resolves the PaymentIntent, but a fully gift-funded job has none and takes the 'no payment found' branch: it settles payment_status 'cancelled' with the whole gift restored and pays no fee, and Parts D/E only retry jobs whose payment_status is 'refunded' with the fee 'charged'. So a Helpr (or crew) cancelled on late is owed a fee nobody pays. Fix to weigh: pay the fee from the gift (restore the gift less the fee, as Q1490 does for block fees, and pay it from the platform balance with a ledger row), with the same no-fee rules as a card-paid job. GUARD when built: a void-cancelled-payments edge case for a gift-funded single job and crew with a late cancel. The same class, from the lh-money-escrow review of Q1490 (#9): a crew paid partly by gift and partly by card whose card capture is smaller than its block fees still restores the whole gift (settleCancelledJob on the card branches), and Part F pays the rest of the fee from the platform balance. finding: lh-money-escrow@e45c44f44#9
- [ ] **Q1782 LOW Two refund-notice gaps left after the parallel fix of the sweep double-send and the failed-refund tax page (found doing those items, 2026-10-12; the same fixes as ready/items-c-refund-notice, PR #2918, were also built on ready/todo-a-1, which is superseded and not landed).** (1) The webhook-first order: when charge.refunded announces a Stripe-listed release as 'Refund issued' BEFORE void-cancelled-payments writes its release notice, the release is still announced too (the same money twice); a fix built and reviewed clean on origin/ready/todo-a-1 (commit 'a re-issued refund gets its own tax reversal; a release a refund notice already announced is not told twice'): sendRefundNotice(kind release) skips when a finished refund:<re> notice for the same payment and amount exists within an hour, failing open on a read error. (2) A full refund re-issued after a failed one reuses the tax reversal's idempotency key and reference refund:<charge>, so Stripe returns the old reversal and nothing is reversed or paged; the same branch keyed the reversal on the refund id instead (#2918 instead tells ops to leave the reversal; check which rule the owner wants). GUARD when built: the refundNotice and stripe-webhook edge cases on that branch. finding: lh-money-escrow@4dfdc0a85#1
